Model before rewriting:

find x: int(5..7)
find y: int(0..3)
find z: int(0..4)

such that

x % y % z != 3

--

x
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
x
new variables:
  find x#sign_magnitude_1: bool
  find x#sign_magnitude_2: bool
  find x#sign_magnitude_3: bool
  find x#sign_magnitude_4: bool
new constraints:
  or([!(x#sign_magnitude_3),or([!(x#sign_magnitude_2),or([!(x#sign_magnitude_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)])
  !(x#sign_magnitude_4)
  (!(x#sign_magnitude_4)) -> (or([!(x#sign_magnitude_3),or([!(x#sign_magnitude_2),or([!(x#sign_magnitude_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)]))
  (x#sign_magnitude_4) -> (or([x#sign_magnitude_1,x#sign_magnitude_2,x#sign_magnitude_3;int(1..)]))
  or([and([SATInt(SignMagnitude, [x#sign_magnitude_1,x#sign_magnitude_2,x#sign_magnitude_3,x#sign_magnitude_4;int(1..)] [5, 7]) >= 5,SATInt(SignMagnitude, [x#sign_magnitude_1,x#sign_magnitude_2,x#sign_magnitude_3,x#sign_magnitude_4;int(1..)] [5, 7]) <= 7;int(1..)]);int(1..)])

--

y
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
y
new variables:
  find y#sign_magnitude_1: bool
  find y#sign_magnitude_2: bool
  find y#sign_magnitude_3: bool
new constraints:
  or([!(y#sign_magnitude_2),or([!(y#sign_magnitude_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)])
  !(y#sign_magnitude_3)
  (!(y#sign_magnitude_3)) -> (or([!(y#sign_magnitude_2),or([!(y#sign_magnitude_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]))
  (y#sign_magnitude_3) -> (or([y#sign_magnitude_1,y#sign_magnitude_2;int(1..)]))
  or([and([SATInt(SignMagnitude, [y#sign_magnitude_1,y#sign_magnitude_2,y#sign_magnitude_3;int(1..)] [0, 3]) >= 0,SATInt(SignMagnitude, [y#sign_magnitude_1,y#sign_magnitude_2,y#sign_magnitude_3;int(1..)] [0, 3]) <= 3;int(1..)]);int(1..)])

--

z
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
z
new variables:
  find z#sign_magnitude_1: bool
  find z#sign_magnitude_2: bool
  find z#sign_magnitude_3: bool
  find z#sign_magnitude_4: bool
new constraints:
  or([!(z#sign_magnitude_3),and([!(z#sign_magnitude_2),and([!(z#sign_magnitude_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)])
  !(z#sign_magnitude_4)
  (!(z#sign_magnitude_4)) -> (or([!(z#sign_magnitude_3),and([!(z#sign_magnitude_2),and([!(z#sign_magnitude_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)]))
  (z#sign_magnitude_4) -> (or([z#sign_magnitude_1,z#sign_magnitude_2,z#sign_magnitude_3;int(1..)]))
  or([and([SATInt(SignMagnitude, [z#sign_magnitude_1,z#sign_magnitude_2,z#sign_magnitude_3,z#sign_magnitude_4;int(1..)] [0, 4]) >= 0,SATInt(SignMagnitude, [z#sign_magnitude_1,z#sign_magnitude_2,z#sign_magnitude_3,z#sign_magnitude_4;int(1..)] [0, 4]) <= 4;int(1..)]);int(1..)])
